# Coverage

Generated by scripts/coverage.mjs; do not edit by hand. Run `pnpm coverage` to write it again.

Every named crest the crest books list (`docs/coverage/kamon.json`), by chapter, each marked as drawn by this package or still missing. 696 of 2,759 are drawn (25.2%). The 696 crests the package has are all counted: any the list does not name is added to it, marked "ours only". Tier 1 is a family's base form and the famous crests, tier 2 the common named variants, tier 3 the rarer ones. The same list is on the demo's [Coverage page](https://johnmorrisdotca.github.io/kamon-crests/coverage/).
